        Skin = true, Laser = true, AnimalPodiums = true,
        DeliveryHitbox = true, StealHitbox = true, AnimalTarget = true,
        MainRoot = true, Floor = true, Spawn = true, Door = true, Hitbox = true,
        Base = true, Wall = true, Roof = true, SecondFloor = true, ThirdFloor = true,
        ["structure base home"] = true, Unlock = true,
    }
    local PLOT_NUKE = {
        FriendPanel = true, Cash = true, Purchases = true,
        PlotSign = true, CashPad = true, InvisibleWalls = true, Multiplier = true,
        LaserHitbox = true, Events = true, Sounds = true,
        Slope = true,
    }
    local BODY_PART = {
        HumanoidRootPart = true, Head = true, Torso = true,
        UpperTorso = true, LowerTorso = true,
        LeftUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, LeftHand = true,
        RightUpperArm = true, RightLowerArm = true, RightHand = true,
        LeftUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, LeftFoot = true,
        RightUpperLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true, RightFoot = true,
        ["Left Arm"] = true, ["Right Arm"] = true,
        ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true,
    }
    local CHAR_KEEP_CLASS = {
        Humanoid = true, Animator = true, Attachment = true,
        Motor6D = true, AnimationConstraint = true,
        BallSocketConstraint = true, Weld = true, WeldConstraint = true,
    }
    local MAP_KEEP = { Ground = true, Ground_Left = true, Ground_Right = true }
    local PART_CLASS = {
        Part = true, MeshPart = true, UnionOperation = true,
        WedgePart = true, CornerWedgePart = true, TrussPart = true,
        SpawnLocation = true, Seat = true, VehicleSeat = true,
    }

    local function underUnlock(d)
        local x = d
        for _ = 1, 12 do
            if not x then return false end
            if x.Name == "Unlock" then return true end
            if x == workspace then return false end
            x = x.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function isMine(inst)
        if not (LP and inst) then return false end
        if inst == LP.Character or inst.Name == LP.Name then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function isVisualJunk(d)
        local cn = d.ClassName
        return cn == "Beam" or cn == "Trail" or cn == "ParticleEmitter"
            or cn == "PointLight" or cn == "SpotLight" or cn == "SurfaceLight"
            or cn == "Fire" or cn == "Smoke" or cn == "Sparkles"
            or cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" or cn == "Highlight"
            or cn == "Decal" or cn == "Texture"
    end
    local function isSkinJunk(d)
        if not d then return false end
        if isVisualJunk(d) then return true end
        return PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] == true and not d.CanCollide
    end
    local function isPet(inst)
        if not inst or inst.ClassName ~= "Model" then return false end
        return inst:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart") ~= nil
            or inst:FindFirstChild("RootPart") ~= nil
    end
    local function stripSkin(skin)
        if not (skin and skin.Parent) then return end
        local dd = skin:GetDescendants()
        local dump = {}
        for i = 1, #dd do
            if isSkinJunk(dd[i]) then dump[#dump + 1] = dd[i] end
        end
        for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
    end
    local function skipLaserStrip(plot)
        local keep = _G.__FlowerHubPriv_KeepPlot
        if not keep then
            return true
        end
        return plot and tostring(plot.Name) == tostring(keep)
    end
    local function skipTimerStrip(plot)
        return skipLaserStrip(plot)
    end
    local function plotOf(d)
        local x = d
        for _ = 1, 16 do
            if not x then return nil end
            local p = x.Parent
            if p and p.Name == "Plots" then return x end
            x = p
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function isRemainingTimeGui(d)
        if not d then return false end
        local x = d
        for _ = 1, 16 do
            if not x then break end
            if x.Name == "RemainingTime" then return true end
            x = x.Parent
        end
        local cn = d.ClassName
        if cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" then
            local hit
            pcall(function()
                hit = d:FindFirstChild("RemainingTime", true)
            end)
            if hit then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function stripPurchasesKeepTimer(purch)
        if not (purch and purch.Parent) then return end
        local kids = purch:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local ch = kids[i]
            if ch.Name ~= "PlotBlock" then
                kill(ch)
            else
                local dd = ch:GetDescendants()
                for j = 1, #dd do
                    local d = dd[j]
                    local cn = d.ClassName
                    if (cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui") and not isRemainingTimeGui(d) then
                        kill(d)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local laserPend = {}
    local function stripLaser(laser)
        if not (laser and laser.Parent) then return end
        if skipLaserStrip(laser.Parent) then return end
        local bars = {}
        local beamPart = {}
        local dd = laser:GetDescendants()
        for i = 1, #dd do
            local d = dd[i]
            if d.ClassName == "Beam" then
                local a0, a1 = d.Attachment0, d.Attachment1
                local p0 = a0 and a0.Parent
                local p1 = a1 and a1.Parent
                if p0 and p0:IsA("BasePart") then beamPart[p0] = true end
                if p1 and p1:IsA("BasePart") then beamPart[p1] = true end
            elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
                bars[#bars + 1] = d
            end
        end
        if #bars > 0 then
            local chosen = {}
            for i = 1, #bars do
                if beamPart[bars[i]] then chosen[#chosen + 1] = bars[i] end
            end
            if #chosen == 0 then
                for i = 1, #bars do
                    local d = bars[i]
                    local s = d.Size
                    local mx = math.max(s.X, s.Y, s.Z)
                    local mn = math.min(s.X, s.Y, s.Z)
                    local neon = false
                    pcall(function()
                        neon = d.Material == Enum.Material.Neon
                            or d.Material == Enum.Material.ForceField
                    end)
                    if neon or (mn <= 1.5 and mx >= 3) then
                        chosen[#chosen + 1] = d
                    end
                end
            end
            if #chosen == 0 then chosen = bars end
            local keep = chosen[1]
            if keep then
                for i = 1, #bars do
                    if bars[i] ~= keep then kill(bars[i]) end
                end
                pcall(function()
                    if PART_CLASS[laser.ClassName] then
                        laser.CanCollide = false
                        laser.CanTouch = false
                    end
                    keep.CanCollide = false
                    keep.CanTouch = false
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    local function queueStripLaser(laser)
        if not laser or laserPend[laser] then return end
        laserPend[laser] = true
        task.defer(function()
            laserPend[laser] = nil
            stripLaser(laser)
        end)
    end
    local function stripPetVfx(pet)
        if not pet then return end
        local dd = pet:GetDescendants()
        local dump = {}
        for i = 1, #dd do
            local d = dd[i]
            local n = d.Name
            local cn = d.ClassName
            if n == "VfxInstance" or n == "_VfxInstance" or n == "AnimationController"
                or cn == "ParticleEmitter" or cn == "Beam" or cn == "Trail"
                or cn == "Fire" or cn == "Smoke" or cn == "Sparkles"
                or cn == "Animator" or cn == "AnimationController"
                or cn == "Bone"
                or cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" then
                dump[#dump + 1] = d
            end
        end
        for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
    end
    _G.FlowerHubPriv_StripPetVfx = stripPetVfx

    -- Esconde apenas localmente, sem destruir o modelo. Assim, ao selecionar
    -- outro alvo depois, o bicho antigo pode sumir e o novo pode reaparecer.
    local function setPetLocalVisible(pet, visible)
        if not pet then return end
        -- RootPart/FakeRootPart sao hitboxes quadrados usados pelo jogo para
        -- posicionar o brainrot; nao fazem parte do modelo visual.
        local function isVisualBlock(d)
            local n = string.lower(tostring(d.Name or ""))
            return n == "fakerootpart" or n == "rootpart"
                or n == "humanoidrootpart" or n == "hitbox"
                or n == "__hitbox" or n == "proxyvisual"
        end
        for _, d in ipairs(pet:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("BasePart") then
                pcall(function()
                    local block = isVisualBlock(d)
                    d.LocalTransparencyModifier = (visible and not block) and 0 or 1
                    -- Alguns modelos ficam com Transparency=1 depois do
                    -- semi-invisivel; o alvo selecionado precisa reaparecer.
                    if visible and not block then d.Transparency = 0 end
                    if block then d.Transparency = 1; d.CanCollide = false end
                end)
            elseif d:IsA("BillboardGui") or d:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                -- Mostrar somente o modelo, sem nome/valor/raridade.
                pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end)
            end
        end
    end
    _G.FlowerHubPrivSetPetVisible = setPetLocalVisible

    -- Bicho fica se estiver na lista Priority OU for 10M+ (so o modelo; sem nome/valor/mutacao)
    local function petKept(plot, ch)
        local set = _G.FlowerHubPriv_PrioSet
        if type(set) ~= "table" then return true end
        -- Alvo travado tem prioridade absoluta, mesmo enquanto o scanner
        -- ainda esta atualizando durante o semi-invisivel.
        local targetUid = type(_G.TT3StealTargetUID) == "string" and _G.TT3StealTargetUID or ""
        if targetUid ~= "" then
            local targetSlot = targetUid:match("_(.+)$")
            local probe = ch
            while probe and probe.Parent and probe.Parent.Name ~= "AnimalPodiums" do probe = probe.Parent end
            if targetSlot and probe and tostring(probe.Name) == tostring(targetSlot) then
                setPetLocalVisible(ch, true)
                return true
            end
        end
        if set[string.lower(ch.Name)] == true then setPetLocalVisible(ch, true); return true end
        -- O nome do Model pode ser diferente do DisplayName. Resolva pelo
        -- slot do podium para que o melhor brainrot nao seja destruido.
        local podium = ch
        while podium and podium.Parent and podium.Parent.Name ~= "AnimalPodiums" do
            podium = podium.Parent
        end
        if podium and podium.Parent and _G.FlowerHubPrivPetEligibleSlot then
            local okSlot, keepSlot = pcall(_G.FlowerHubPrivPetEligibleSlot,
                plot and plot.Name, podium.Name)
            if okSlot and keepSlot then setPetLocalVisible(ch, true); return true end
        end
        local f = _G.FlowerHubPrivPetEligible
        if f then
            local okF, r = pcall(f, plot and plot.Name, ch.Name)
            if okF and r then setPetLocalVisible(ch, true); return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function killPetUnlessKept(plot, ch)
        if petKept(plot, ch) then stripPetVfx(ch); return end
        if _G.FlowerHubPrivScanReady == true then setPetLocalVisible(ch, false); return end
        -- ainda sem scan de valores: espera antes de decidir, sem destruir
        task.delay(2.5, function()
            if not (ch and ch.Parent) then return end
            if petKept(plot, ch) then stripPetVfx(ch) else setPetLocalVisible(ch, false) end
        end)
    end

    local function isCharJunk(d)
        if not d then return false end
        local cn = d.ClassName
        if cn == "PathfindingModifier" then return false end
        if cn == "WrapTarget" or cn == "WrapLayer" then return true end
        if cn == "Shirt" or cn == "ShirtGraphic" then return true end
        if cn == "NoCollisionConstraint" then return true end
        local nm = d.Name
        if nm == "__HITBOX" or nm == "OriginalPosition" or nm == "OriginalSize" then return true end
        if CHAR_KEEP_CLASS[cn] then return false end
        if BODY_PART[nm] then return false end
        return true
    end
    local function isUuidName(n)
        return type(n) == "string" and #n == 36
            and string.match(n, "^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$") ~= nil
    end
    local function isOtherAvatar(char)
        if not (char and char.ClassName == "Model") then return false end
        if isMine(char) then return false end
        if isPet(char) then return false end
        local n = char.Name
        if type(n) == "string" and string.sub(n, -6) == "_Clone" then return false end
        if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) then return true end
        if Players:FindFirstChild(n) then return true end
        return char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") ~= nil
    end
    local hookedChars = {}
    local function stripOtherChar(char)
        if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
        if not (char and char.Parent) or isMine(char) or not isOtherAvatar(char) then return end
        local function sweep()
            local dd = char:GetDescendants()
            local dump = {}
            for i = 1, #dd do
                if isCharJunk(dd[i]) then dump[#dump + 1] = dd[i] end
            end
            for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
        end
        sweep()
        if hookedChars[char] then return end
        hookedChars[char] = true
        char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
            if d and d.Parent and not isMine(char) and isCharJunk(d) then kill(d) end
        end)
    end

    local strippedPlots = {}
    _G.__FlowerHubPriv_StrippedPlots = strippedPlots
    local function near(a, b)
        return math.abs(a - b) < 0.2
    end
    local function isPlotPadPart(d)
        if not d or PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] ~= true then return false end
        if d.Name == "structure base home" or d.Name == "Spawn" then return false end
        local s = d.Size
        if near(s.X, 4) and near(s.Y, 1) and near(s.Z, 4) then return true end
        if near(s.X, 2) and near(s.Y, 0.5) and near(s.Z, 2) then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function isPodiumPlate(d)
        if not d or PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] ~= true then return false end
        if d.Name == "Spawn" then return false end
        local s = d.Size
        if near(s.X, 6) and near(s.Y, 0.2) and near(s.Z, 6) then return true end
        if near(s.X, 4) and near(s.Y, 0.2) and near(s.Z, 4) then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function stripPlotDecor(dec)
        if not (dec and dec.Parent) then return end
        local dump = {}
        local function walk(x)
            local kids = x:GetChildren()
            for i = 1, #kids do
                local d = kids[i]
                local cn = d.ClassName
                local nm = d.Name
                if nm == "Spawn" or nm == "PromptAttachment" or cn == "ProximityPrompt" then
                elseif cn == "Model" and isPet(d) then
                elseif cn == "Beam" or cn == "PointLight" then
                    dump[#dump + 1] = d
                elseif isPlotPadPart(d) then
                    dump[#dump + 1] = d
                else
                    walk(d)
                end
            end
        end
        walk(dec)
        for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
    end
    local function stripPodiumPlates(pods)
        if not (pods and pods.Parent) then return end
        local dump = {}
        local function scan(folder)
            if not folder then return end
            local dd = folder:GetDescendants()
            for i = 1, #dd do
                local d = dd[i]
                if d.Name ~= "Spawn" and d.Name ~= "PromptAttachment"
                    and d.ClassName ~= "ProximityPrompt" and isPodiumPlate(d) then
                    dump[#dump + 1] = d
                end
            end
        end
        local slots = pods:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #slots do
            local slot = slots[i]
            local base = slot:FindFirstChild("Base")
            if base then scan(base:FindFirstChild("Decorations")) end
            scan(slot:FindFirstChild("Decorations"))
        end
        for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
    end
    local function handlePlotChild(plot, ch)
        if not (ch and ch.Parent) then return end
        local n = ch.Name
        if n == "Skin" then stripSkin(ch); return end
        if n == "Laser" then
            if not skipLaserStrip(plot) then stripLaser(ch) end
            return
        end
        if n == "AnimalPodiums" then stripPodiumPlates(ch); return end
        if n == "Decorations" then stripPlotDecor(ch); return end
        if n == "Purchases" then
            if skipTimerStrip(plot) then
                stripPurchasesKeepTimer(ch)
            else
                kill(ch)
            end
            return
        end
        if PLOT_KEEP[n] then return end
        if PLOT_NUKE[n] then kill(ch); return end
        if isPet(ch) then
            killPetUnlessKept(plot, ch)
            return
        end
    end
    local function stripPlotInteriorFast(plot)
        if not (plot and plot.Parent) then return end
        if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot and plot.Name == _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot then return end
        strippedPlots[plot.Name] = true
        local kids = plot:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local ch = kids[i]
            if ch and ch.Parent then
                local n = ch.Name
                if n == "Skin" or n == "Laser" or n == "AnimalPodiums" or n == "Decorations" then
                elseif n == "Purchases" and skipTimerStrip(plot) then
                    stripPurchasesKeepTimer(ch)
                elseif PLOT_KEEP[n] then
                elseif PLOT_NUKE[n] then
                    kill(ch)
                elseif isPet(ch) then
                    killPetUnlessKept(plot, ch)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function stripPlotVisual(plot)
        if not (plot and plot.Parent) then return end
        if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot and plot.Name == _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot then return end
        local skin = plot:FindFirstChild("Skin")
        if skin then stripSkin(skin) end
        local laser = plot:FindFirstChild("Laser")
        if laser and not skipLaserStrip(plot) then stripLaser(laser) end
        local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if pods then stripPodiumPlates(pods) end
        local dec = plot:FindFirstChild("Decorations")
        if dec then stripPlotDecor(dec) end
        local kids = plot:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local ch = kids[i]
            if isPet(ch) then stripPetVfx(ch) end
        end
    end
    local function stripPlotInterior(plot)
        stripPlotInteriorFast(plot)
        stripPlotVisual(plot)
    end
    local function connectPlot(plot)
        if not (plot and plot.Parent) then return end
        plot.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
            handlePlotChild(plot, ch)
        end)
        plot.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
            if not d then return end
            if underUnlock(d) then return end
            local cn = d.ClassName
            if cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" then
                if skipTimerStrip(plot) and isRemainingTimeGui(d) then return end
                kill(d)
                return
            end
            if d.Name == "Spawn" or d.Name == "PromptAttachment" or cn == "ProximityPrompt" then
                return
            end
            if d.Name == "Decorations" and d.Parent == plot then
                stripPlotDecor(d)
                return
            end
            local p = d.Parent
            if not p then return end
            if p.Name == "LaserHitbox" then kill(d); return end
            if PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] then
                if isPlotPadPart(d) then
                    local x = p
                    local inPlotDec, skip = false, false
                    while x and x ~= plot do
                        if isPet(x) or x.Name == "Spawn" or x.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                            skip = true; break
                        end
                        if x.Name == "Decorations" and x.Parent == plot then inPlotDec = true end
                        x = x.Parent
                    end
                    if inPlotDec and not skip then kill(d); return end
                elseif isPodiumPlate(d) then
                    local x = p
                    local inDec, skip = false, false
                    while x and x ~= plot do
                        if x.Name == "Spawn" then skip = true; break end
                        if x.Name == "Decorations" then inDec = true end
                        if x.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                            if inDec and not skip then kill(d) end
                            return
                        end
                        x = x.Parent
                    end
                end
            end
            local sk = p
            while sk and sk ~= plot do
                if sk.Name == "Skin" then
                    if isSkinJunk(d) then kill(d) end
                    return
                end
                sk = sk.Parent
            end
            local las = p
            while las and las ~= plot do
                if las.Name == "Laser" then
                    if skipLaserStrip(plot) then return end
                    if PART_CLASS[d.ClassName] then
                        pcall(function()
                            d.CanCollide = false
                            d.CanTouch = false
                        end)
                    end
                    queueStripLaser(las)
                    return
                end
                las = las.Parent
            end
        end)
    end
    local function plotIsMine(plot)
        if not plot then return false end
        if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot and plot.Name == _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot then return true end
        local ch
        pcall(function()
            if _G.FlowerHubPrivSyncGet then ch = _G.FlowerHubPrivSyncGet(plot.Name) end
        end)
        if ch and _G.FlowerHubPriv_IsMyPlot and _G.FlowerHubPriv_IsMyPlot(ch) then
            _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot = plot.Name
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    local function canDestroyPlot(plot)
        if not plot then return false end
        if plotIsMine(plot) then return false end
        local keep = _G.__FlowerHubPriv_KeepPlot
        if keep and plot.Name == keep then return false end
        if not _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot then return false end
        return plot.Name ~= _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot
    end
    local hookedPlots
    local function hookPlot(plot)
        if plotIsMine(plot) then return end
        stripPlotInterior(plot)
        connectPlot(plot)
    end
    local function hookPlots(folder)
        if not folder or hookedPlots == folder then return end
        hookedPlots = folder
        local kids = folder:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            if not plotIsMine(kids[i]) then
                stripPlotInterior(kids[i])
                connectPlot(kids[i])
            end
        end
        folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(plot)
            hookPlot(plot)
            if canDestroyPlot(plot) then
                kill(plot)
            end
        end)
    end
    _G.FlowerHubPriv_LockPrioPlot = function(plotName)
        if not plotName then return end
        _G.__FlowerHubPriv_KeepPlot = tostring(plotName)
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end
        local kids = plots:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local plot = kids[i]
            if canDestroyPlot(plot) then
                kill(plot)
            end
        end
        local mine = _G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot and plots:FindFirstChild(_G.__FlowerHubPriv_MyPlot)
        if mine and _G.FlowerHubPriv_NukePlotPodiums then
            pcall(_G.FlowerHubPriv_NukePlotPodiums, mine)
        end
        local keepPlot = _G.__FlowerHubPriv_KeepPlot and plots:FindFirstChild(_G.__FlowerHubPriv_KeepPlot)
        if keepPlot and _G.FlowerHubPriv_StripNonPrioPodiums then
            pcall(_G.FlowerHubPriv_StripNonPrioPodiums, keepPlot)
        end
    end

    local function stripMap(map)
        local function handle(x)
            if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
            if x and not MAP_KEEP[x.Name] then kill(x) end
        end
        local kids = map:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do handle(kids[i]) end
        map.ChildAdded:Connect(handle)
    end
    -- Enquanto voce carrega um brainrot (atributo Stealing) e ate 3s depois, o
    -- modelo carregado NAO pode ser destruido, senao ele nao aparece na mao.
    local function _carrying()
        return LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
            or os.clock() < (_G.__FlowerHubPriv_CarryUntil or 0)
    end
    LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
            _G.__FlowerHubPriv_CarryUntil = os.clock() + 3
        end
    end)
    local function killUnlessCarried(x)
        -- pequeno atraso: o atributo Stealing pode chegar depois do modelo
        task.delay(0.3, function()
            if x and x.Parent and not _carrying() then kill(x) end
        end)
    end
    local hookedRma
    local function hookRma(rma)
        if not rma or hookedRma == rma then return end
        hookedRma = rma
        local function handle(x)
            if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
            killUnlessCarried(x)
        end
        local kids = rma:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do handle(kids[i]) end
        rma.ChildAdded:Connect(handle)
    end
    local function onWsChild(ch)
        if not ch then return end
        if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
        local n = ch.Name
        if ch.ClassName == "Camera" or ch.ClassName == "Terrain" then return end
        if n == "Plots" then hookPlots(ch); return end
        if n == "RenderedMovingAnimals" then return end   -- nao oculta mais os brainrots (mutacao/traits/valor/raridade visiveis)
        if n == "Map" then stripMap(ch); return end
        if isMine(ch) then return end
        if isUuidName(n) then return end   -- nao oculta mais os brainrots
        if isOtherAvatar(ch) then stripOtherChar(ch); return end
        if ch.ClassName == "Model" then
            ch.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
                if isOtherAvatar(ch) then stripOtherChar(ch) end
            end)
        end
        if string.sub(n, 1, 7) == "BeeHive" or string.sub(n, 1, 7) == "Sentry_" then
            kill(ch)
            return
        end
        if WS_KILL[n] or ch.ClassName == "Highlight" then kill(ch); return end
        if ch.ClassName == "Model" and string.find(n, "'s Rainbow", 1, true) then
            kill(ch)
        end
    end
    local wsch = workspace:GetChildren()
    local plotsFolder, mapFolder, rmaFolder
    local rest = {}
    for i = 1, #wsch do
        local ch = wsch[i]
        local n = ch.Name
        if n == "Plots" then plotsFolder = ch
        elseif n == "Map" then mapFolder = ch
        elseif n == "RenderedMovingAnimals" then rmaFolder = ch
        else rest[#rest + 1] = ch end
    end
    if mapFolder then stripMap(mapFolder) end
    for i = 1, #rest do onWsChild(rest[i]) end
    if plotsFolder then hookPlots(plotsFolder) end
    workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(onWsChild)

    local function hookPlrChar(plr)
        if not plr or plr == LP then return end
        if plr.Character then stripOtherChar(plr.Character) end
        plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(stripOtherChar)
    end
    local plrs = Players:GetPlayers()
    for i = 1, #plrs do hookPlrChar(plrs[i]) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookPlrChar)

    local function onLit(x)
        if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
        if not x then return end
        local cn = x.ClassName
        if cn == "Sky" or cn == "Atmosphere" or cn == "Clouds"
            or cn == "SunRaysEffect" or cn == "ColorCorrectionEffect" then
            kill(x)
        end
    end
    local lit = Lighting:GetChildren()
    for i = 1, #lit do onLit(lit[i]) end
    Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(onLit)
    local function stripCamFx(x)
        if not x then return end
        local cn = x.ClassName
        if cn == "ColorCorrectionEffect" or cn == "BlurEffect" then kill(x) end
    end
    local hookedCam
    local function hookCam(cam)
        if not cam or hookedCam == cam then return end
        hookedCam = cam
        local ck = cam:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #ck do stripCamFx(ck[i]) end
        cam.ChildAdded:Connect(stripCamFx)
    end
    hookCam(workspace.CurrentCamera)
    workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
        hookCam(workspace.CurrentCamera)
    end)
    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if not d then return end
        if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
        local cn = d.ClassName
        if cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" or cn == "Decal" or cn == "Texture" then
            if underUnlock(d) then return end
            do -- infos dos brainrots (mutacao/traits/valor/raridade) nao sao mais removidas
                local anc = d.Parent
                while anc and anc ~= workspace do
                    if anc.Name == "RenderedMovingAnimals" or isUuidName(anc.Name) then return end
                    anc = anc.Parent
                end
            end
            if (cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui")
                and skipTimerStrip(plotOf(d)) and isRemainingTimeGui(d) then
                return
            end
            kill(d)
        end
    end)
    if mapFolder and mapFolder.Parent then
        local md = mapFolder:GetDescendants()
        for i = 1, #md do
            local cn = md[i].ClassName
            if cn == "Decal" or cn == "Texture" then kill(md[i]) end
        end
    end
    do
        local ter = workspace.Terrain
        pcall(function() ter.Decoration = false end)
        pcall(function() ter:Clear() end)
        local kids = ter:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do kill(kids[i]) end
        ter.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
            kill(ch)
        end)
        ter.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if _G.__FlowerHubPriv_VelFlying then return end
            if d then kill(d) end
        end)
    end
    _G.__FlowerHubPriv_StripDone = true
end

-- aliases de compatibilidade para o Hub em edicao
_G.FlowerHubPrivSyncGet = _G.FlowerHubPrivSyncGet or _G.TT3SyncGet
_G.FlowerHubPriv_IsMyPlot = _G.FlowerHubPriv_IsMyPlot or function(ch)
    local ok, r = pcall(function() return isMyPlot(ch) end)
    return ok and r == true
end
_G.FlowerHubPriv_PrioSet = _G.FlowerHubPriv_PrioSet or {}
for _, n in ipairs(_G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS or {}) do
    if type(n) == "string" then _G.FlowerHubPriv_PrioSet[string.lower(n)] = true end
end

task.defer(function()
    pcall(__FlowerHubPrivStrip)
end)


-- ============================================================
-- FLOWER HUB PRIV / ROSE VISUAL TRANSPLANT
-- Reuses the Hub scanner already defined above. Visuals are intentionally filtered:
-- 10M+/s or an item present in the active Priority list. No map/base/machine rebuild.
-- ============================================================
do
    local _Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local _Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local _LP = _Players.LocalPlayer
    local _PG = _LP and _LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    local _MIN = tonumber(_G.TT3AutoGrabMinGen) or 5000000
    local _visual = {}
    local _brainBills = {}
    local _carpetBill
    local _folder = _Workspace:FindFirstChild("FlowerHubPrivRoseESP")
    if not _folder then _folder = Instance.new("Folder"); _folder.Name = "FlowerHubPrivRoseESP"; _folder.Parent = _Workspace end

    local function _priority(a)
        local n = a and (a.name or a.index)
        return n and _priIndexOf and _priIndexOf(n) ~= nil or false
    end
    local function _isTarget(a)
        return a and type(_G.TT3StealTargetUID) == "string"
            and (tostring(a.plot) .. "_" .. tostring(a.slot)) == _G.TT3StealTargetUID
    end
    local function _isBest(a)
        -- _visual e ordenado por MPS antes de _eligible ser chamado.
        return a and _visual[1] == a
    end
    local function _hasTarget()
        return type(_G.TT3StealTargetUID) == "string" and _G.TT3StealTargetUID ~= ""
    end
    local function _eligible(a)
        -- Com um alvo selecionado, somente ele fica visivel. Sem selecao,
        -- mantem-se o comportamento anterior: melhor/prioridade/valor alto.
        if _hasTarget() then return a and _isTarget(a) end
        return a and (_isBest(a) or (tonumber(a.mps or a.genValue) or 0) >= _MIN or _priority(a))
    end
    _G.FlowerHubPrivPetEligible = function(plotName, petName)
        for _, a in ipairs(_visual) do
            if a.plot == plotName and a.name == petName and _eligible(a) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    _G.FlowerHubPrivPetEligibleSlot = function(plotName, slot)
        for _, a in ipairs(_visual) do
            if tostring(a.plot) == tostring(plotName)
                and tostring(a.slot) == tostring(slot) then
                return _eligible(a)
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    -- Destaque so do bicho (atravessa parede), sem nome/valor/mutacao.
    local _hls = {}
    local function _highlight(model, isPrio, isTarget)
        -- Sem destaque colorido: o pedido e mostrar somente o bicho.
        local h = _hls[model] or (model and model:FindFirstChild("FlowerHubPrivAnimalHL"))
        if h then pcall(function() h:Destroy() end) end
        _hls[model] = nil
    end
    local function _sweepHighlights(want)
        for model, h in pairs(_hls) do
            local carpetModel = _carpetBill and _carpetBill.Parent
            if (not want[model] and model ~= carpetModel) or not (model and model.Parent) then
                pcall(function() h:Destroy() end)
                _hls[model] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local function _petModel(a)
        local plots = _Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(tostring(a.plot))
        if not plot then return nil end
        -- Resolve por slot; procurar somente pelo nome falha com duplicatas.
        local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        local podium = podiums and podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(a.slot))
        if podium then
            for _, d in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("Model") and d.Name ~= "Claim" and d.Name ~= "Base"
                    and d.Name ~= "Decorations"
                    and (d:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart") or d:FindFirstChild("RootPart")) then return d end
            end
        end
        local m = plot:FindFirstChild(tostring(a.name))
        if m and m:IsA("Model") then return m end
        return nil
    end
    local function _genFromText(t)
        if type(t) ~= "string" then return 0 end
        local x = t:lower():gsub("[%$,/s%s]", "")
        local n = tonumber(x:match("([%d%.]+)")); if not n then return 0 end
        if x:find("b") then return n*1e9 elseif x:find("m") then return n*1e6 elseif x:find("k") then return n*1e3 end
        return n
    end
    local function _part(a)
        if a and a.position then return nil, a.position end
        if getPetPosition and a and a.plot and a.slot then
            local ok,pos=pcall(getPetPosition,_Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") and _Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(a.plot),a.slot)
            if ok and pos then return nil,pos end
        end
        return nil,nil
    end
    local function _clearBrain()
        for _,b in pairs(_brainBills) do pcall(function() b:Destroy() end) end
        table.clear(_brainBills)
    end
    local function _bill(name, pos, gen, color)
        local bb=Instance.new("BillboardGui"); bb.Name="RoseBrainrotESP"; bb.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(180,42)
        bb.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,2.2,0); bb.AlwaysOnTop=true; bb.MaxDistance=3000
        local root=Instance.new("Frame"); root.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1); root.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(8,8,8); root.BackgroundTransparency=.35; root.BorderSizePixel=0; root.Parent=bb
        Instance.new("UICorner",root).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7)
        local st=Instance.new("UIStroke",root); st.Color=color; st.Thickness=1.5; st.Transparency=.15
        local n=Instance.new("TextLabel",root); n.BackgroundTransparency=1; n.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,20); n.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(4,2); n.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; n.TextSize=12; n.TextColor3=color; n.Text=name or "Brainrot"; n.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        local g=Instance.new("TextLabel",root); g.BackgroundTransparency=1; g.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,16); g.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(4,23); g.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; g.TextSize=11; g.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235); g.Text=gen or ""; g.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        return bb
    end
    local function _refreshBrain()
        _clearBrain()
        local ok,all=pcall(scanAllPets)
        if not ok or type(all)~="table" then return end
        _visual=all
        _G.FlowerHubPrivScanReady = true
        table.sort(all,function(a,b) return (tonumber(a.mps) or 0)>(tonumber(b.mps) or 0) end)
        local want = {}
        for _,a in ipairs(all) do
            local keep = _eligible(a)
            local model = _petModel(a)
            if model and _G.FlowerHubPrivSetPetVisible then
                pcall(_G.FlowerHubPrivSetPetVisible, model, keep)
            end
            if keep then
                if model then
                    want[model] = true
                    _highlight(model, _priority(a), _isTarget(a))
                end
            end
        end
        _sweepHighlights(want)
    end
    _G.FlowerHubPrivScanAll=function()
        local ok,r=pcall(scanAllPets); return ok and r or {} end
    _G.FlowerHubPrivVisualAnimals=function() return _visual end
    _G.FlowerHubPrivRefreshVisuals = _refreshBrain
    -- Reforco durante o semi-invisivel: o alvo continua visivel mesmo estando
    -- embaixo/fora da camera, e a regiao e pedida ao streaming uma vez por alvo.
    task.spawn(function()
        local lastRequested
        while _folder and _folder.Parent do
            task.wait(0.2)
            local uid = _G.TT3StealTargetUID
            if type(uid) == "string" and uid ~= "" then
                -- Nao dependa somente de _visual: no instante em que o
                -- semi-invisivel liga, o scanner pode estar entre dois ciclos.
                -- Resolva o alvo pelo plot/slot e force a visibilidade local.
                local plotName, slotName = uid:match("^(.*)_(.+)$")
                local plots = _Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
                local plot = plots and plotName and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
                local podiums = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                local podium = podiums and slotName and podiums:FindFirstChild(slotName)
                if podium then
                    for _, d in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("Model") and (d:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart") or d:FindFirstChild("RootPart")) then
                            if _G.FlowerHubPrivSetPetVisible then pcall(_G.FlowerHubPrivSetPetVisible, d, true) end
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                for _, a in ipairs(_visual) do
                    if _isTarget(a) then
                        local model = _petModel(a)
                        if model and model.Parent then
                            if lastRequested ~= uid then
                                lastRequested = uid
                                pcall(function()
                                    local pos = model:GetPivot().Position
                                    _LP:RequestStreamAroundAsync(pos)
                                end)
                            end
                            if _G.FlowerHubPrivSetPetVisible then
                                pcall(_G.FlowerHubPrivSetPetVisible, model, true)
                            end
                            _highlight(model, _priority(a), true)
                        end
                        break
                    end
                end
            else
                lastRequested = nil
            end
        end
    end)
    -- Carpet/conveyor ESP: copied behavior, but only the best visible item at 10M+ or priority.
    local function _clearCarpet()
        if _carpetBill then
            local m = _carpetBill.Parent
            pcall(function() _carpetBill:Destroy() end)
            if m then _hls[m] = nil end
            _carpetBill=nil
        end
    end
    local function _refreshCarpet()
        local best,bestG,bestPart=nil,0,nil
        for _,d in ipairs(_Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d.Enabled and tostring(d.ActionText):lower():find("purchase") then
                local p=d.Parent; local part=p and (p:IsA("Attachment") and p.Parent or p)
                if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local g=0
                    for _,x in ipairs(part.Parent:GetDescendants()) do if x:IsA("TextLabel") then g=math.max(g,_genFromText(x.Text)) end end
                    if g>=_MIN and g>bestG then best,bestG,bestPart=d,g,part end
                end
            end
        end
        if not bestPart then _clearCarpet(); return end
        local cm = bestPart:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
        if cm and not cm:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            if _carpetBill and _carpetBill.Parent ~= cm then _clearCarpet() end
            _highlight(cm, true)
            _carpetBill = cm:FindFirstChild("FlowerHubPrivAnimalHL")
        else
            _clearCarpet()
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(1.0); pcall(_refreshBrain) end end)
    task.spawn(function() while true do task.wait(0.8); pcall(_refreshCarpet) end end)
    task.defer(function() pcall(_refreshBrain); pcall(_refreshCarpet) end)
end
